# Primary sources

Primary sources about Kowloon Walled City (九龍城寨), with a focus on the
city at peak density, from about 1985 to the clearance in 1991–1993. The
model must use these sources for its facts.

A source is primary if it was made at the time, by a person or body that saw
the city or ran its clearance. Use the usual meaning that historians give the
term. First-hand accounts given later (oral history, memoirs) also count, but
tag them "retrospective (year)". Books and articles that tell the story later
are not primary. Do not put them here.

The images and scans of both source trees are listed by date in
[`../docs/source-images.md`](../docs/source-images.md).

## Conventions

- One folder for each source.
- Each folder has a `SOURCE.md`: citation, link, retrieval date, license, why
  the source is primary, the key facts with page numbers, and checksums.
- Each source has one main link in the table below.
- Open-licensed files go in the folder and git stores them.
- Copyrighted files go in `<folder>/local/`. Git ignores every `local/`
  folder (see `/.gitignore`). These files are on John's Mac only. The
  `SOURCE.md` gives the URL and the SHA-256, so you can get the same file again.
- Copies are byte-identical to what the host gives. Do not crop, convert or
  re-encode them. If a host gives only a derived file, say so in `SOURCE.md`.
- Before a commit, run `git status --ignored primary-sources` and make sure
  that no `local/` file is staged. Then run `node tools/check_source_files.mjs`: the repo is
  public, and the check fails if a file in git has no open licence in its `SOURCE.md` (the
  "Stored in git" line). A file under an NC, ND or NoAI licence stays in `local/`.
- If facts in `data/room_research.json` come from a source, its `SOURCE.md`
  ends with their ids (section "Room facts").
- A folder or a section marked "catalogued, not read" lists only the items that the
  source search of 2026-11 found. Read each item on its source before you use one of its facts.

The LA Times, NYT, AP, Chicago Tribune, Guardian and Independent block this
tool's crawler. A library database (ProQuest, NewsBank) gives access to their
1984–1993 reports. ProQuest Historical Newspapers also has the SCMP archive.

## Other leads not yet collected

**Plans and surveys**
- The 1987 pre-clearance survey by the HK government, which counted residents,
  businesses, doctors and dentists. No copy online found yet.
- PWD plan C-180-N7-1 (Jan 1974), which showed 563 buildings. Known only from
  search extracts on hkintexts.histsyn.com.
- The base plan under the Suenn Ho route maps. Maker and date unknown.
- The plan on display in Kowloon Walled City Park (九龍寨城公園), which `data/plan.json` is
  traced from. Maker and date unknown. Not yet confirmed as primary. Check
  *City of Darkness Revisited* for its origin.
- HK Lands Department (地政總署) (Survey and Mapping Office (測繪處)) aerial photos of the 1980s.
  They are for sale at full resolution. The free Aerial Photo Flight Index
  gives each frame's date and position:
  https://data.gov.hk/en-data/dataset/hk-landsd-openmap-aerial-photo-flight-index
- Lands Department old survey sheets, free GeoTIFF open data. The city is on
  1:1000 sheet **11-NE-11A** (HK1980 Grid E 837500–838250, N 821000–821600)
  and 1:600 sheet **180-NW-1**. The 1:600 sheet number matches the 1974 PWD
  plan "C-180-NW-1". Get the 1980s–1990s editions from HKMS 2.0, series
  "1:1 000 (1960s - current)": https://www.hkmapservice.gov.hk/OneStopSystem/map-search?product=OSSCatB&series=AB
- Lands Department Digital Orthophoto DOP1000-1982 (and 1976, 1963), on HKMS
  2.0 under "Aerial Photo And Image Product". Check the price and the license.
- USGS declassified KH-9 satellite images, 1971–84, public domain. Search
  EarthExplorer at 22.3321 N, 114.1903 E.
- GRS Search Room files (see `grs-catalogue-kwc/`): HKRS2298-1-171 floor
  areas of structures (1990–93), HKRS2704-1-1 lots (1987–91), HKRS1078-1-50
  water supply (1987–92). Physical only, in Kwun Tong (觀塘).
- Pascale Liotard, "Kowloon Walled City: A unique phenomenon comes to an end",
  *PACE Interior Architecture* 39 (1992), 55–62, with maps and plans. At the
  CUHK (香港中文大學) Architecture Library. https://hkapi.lib.cuhk.edu.hk/handle/msO8s77aBT/6111
- Chan Cheung Chuen's (陳祥全) own map of the streets and upper walkways, drawn before
  the demolition (see `retrospective-testimony-web/`). WSJ says he finished it
  in March 1987, after four months (see `wsj-kwc-2014/`). Ask M+.
- HK Yellow Pages 1980s–1992 and the 1981 and 1986 census street-block tables:
  business addresses and households by block. Physical, at HK libraries.
- 『大図解 九龍城』 (Iwanami Shoten, 1997), drawings by 寺澤一美 Terasawa Hitomi:
  see `daizukai-kowloon-1997/`. The web shows only panorama pages 5–6 and
  11–12. The rest of the book, with the room plans of the team's survey, is
  print only. One review says the team's photos show interiors that were
  almost empty, thus after the clearance. Buy a used copy.
  https://www.kinokuniya.co.jp/f/dsg-01-9784000080705
- Michael Chiang, "The Development of the Kowloon Walled City", student
  thesis, HKU School of Architecture, 1979–80. Known only from the JRASHKB
  1987 reference list. No copy online found yet.

**Government files**
- FCO 40/2214 (1987, LegCo Hansards). No link found yet.
- The LegCo Finance Committee (財務委員會) paper of 9 Dec 1987 and the 1993 Director of
  Audit (核數署署長) report on the compensation.

**Photos, film and first-hand reports**
- *City of Darkness: Life in Kowloon Walled City* (Girard and Lambot, 1993):
  photos and resident interviews from 1987–1992. It is the strongest source
  for daily life. It is still in print as *City of Darkness Revisited*, so buy
  it. An archive.org copy exists, but it looks unauthorised, and I did not
  download it.
- Full-size scans of the M+ route maps (CA17/2/1–3) and the M+ photos. Ask M+.
- RTHK (香港電台) documentary "Kowloon Walled City Stories" (九龍城寨的故事) (1988). A copy is in M+ CA17/1/3.
- hkmemory.hk photos of the area, 1968 and 1982.
- Ryuji Miyamoto 宮本隆司, photos of 1987, some from a helicopter. Book
  『九龍城砦』 (Atelier Peyotl (ペヨトル工房), 1988; Heibonsha (平凡社), 1997, ISBN 4582277365).
  https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ry%C5%ABji_Miyamoto
- "Hong Kong's Secret City" (1988, 45 min), Hawke Productions and ORF, with
  Hugo Portisch. Footage from inside the city. No copy found yet.
  https://www.themoviedb.org/movie/416270
- "The Law of Love: The Jackie Pullinger Story" (Channel 4, 1989, dir. Paul
  Lee). Footage from 1989. No copy found yet.
- Jackie Pullinger, *Crack in the Wall: Life and Death in Kowloon Walled
  City* (Hodder & Stoughton, 1989). A memoir of her work in the city from 1966.
- SCMP photo archive. The SCMP gallery "Hong Kong's Kowloon Walled City, from
  the 1970s to demolition" has staff photos. Find the dates and photographers.
- HKU Image Database: "Aerial view of Kowloon City", 1982, three wide scans
  (about 2760×930), and Heather Coulson's photos, about 1986 (about
  3750×2950). Open access, no license stated. Records `bc386k99q`,
  `rv042v600`, `ff3657144`, `xw42tt421`, `66833n60r` at
  https://digitalrepository.lib.hku.hk/catalog/
- Rob Frost, "Kowloon Walled City, Hong Kong. 1990", his own 5-minute video of
  a walk inside: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=TmqlxjTSc8w
- German-language TV documentary, 1988/89, 4 parts, unofficial uploads (can be
  "Hong Kong's Secret City"): https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Lby9P3ms11w
- Films shot at the city: Bloodsport (1987), Shadow of China (1989),
  Yellowthread Street (1990), Baraka (1992, 65 mm, the south face), Crime
  Story (重案組) (1992, in the empty city). Scene notes:
  https://hkmacaufilmtvlocations.blogspot.com/search/label/Kowloon%20Walled%20City
- Whicker's Orient (1972, Alan Whicker), with Gladys Donnithorne.
- PolyU (香港理工大學) Library: Patrick Fong, 27 colour slides of the city, 1988, call no.
  DS796.H76 K68. https://dc.lib.polyu.edu.hk/concern/works/mg74qq83k
- Fiona Hawthorne, *Drawing on the Inside: Kowloon Walled City 1985*
  (Blacksmith Books, 2021): drawings made on site in 1985.
- Paid photo archives: Nik Wheeler 1979/80 roofs (Getty 1219604119), Robert
  Ng / SCMP demolition 23 Mar 1993 (Getty 1126363008), Patrick Zachmann 1987
  (Magnum).
- The six WSJ resident videos of 2014 (52–92 s each). Transcribe them. Links
  are in `wsj-kwc-2014/`.
- Rod A. L. Mason, Hong Kong Police, a report on the city of 1985, made at the
  time. WSJ quotes it (see `wsj-kwc-2014/`). Find the report.
- The Kai Fong Association count of shops, factories and clinics, 1983. WSJ
  charts it (645 in all, 97 dentists). Ask the former residents' association
  (九龍城寨街坊福利事業延續促進會) for the original.
- Gerald Posner, *Warlords of Crime* (1988), chapter 10, "White Powder Ma". A
  visit of 1987 with a police escort. Only an extract is collected.
- SCMP, 23 Mar 2018, "How the dark legacy of the Kowloon Walled City lives on
  in modern-day Hong Kong" (article 2138468). Not yet read. Try a Wayback
  copy. The text of SCMP 2013, article 1191748, is in a copy on The Travel
  Club (see `retrospective-testimony-web/`). The SCMP page itself is blocked.
- 李昇敏 "敏叔", a former resident who guides visitors in Kowloon Walled City
  Park (named in HK01, 20 Jul 2024). A possible new interview.
- 中華書局, 《九龍寨城》 (2026), sample PDF with chapters on Kai Tak and on
  community networks. It cites 《城寨福利會訊》 of Feb 1987 and a 1978 Urban
  Services Department (市政事務署) document. Not yet checked for testimony:
  https://www.chunghwabook.com.hk/upload/files/2026/07_09/8961d31fc1bbfcc1dbd8f8cb3e13d50a.pdf

**Secondary works with useful references**
- Lai, Chua and Chua, "The History of Planning for Kowloon City", *Planning
  Perspectives* 33:1 (2018), 97–112. Read its references for government
  planning records. https://doi.org/10.1080/02665433.2017.1331751

## Leads from the room research (2026-11-05)

The two sweeps of the room research (2026-11-04 and 2026-11-05) did not get
these sources: each one is blocked, print only or offline. `data/room_research.json` (`leads`) gives the full notes.
This list does not repeat the leads in the tables above (Granta 29, The News
Lens, the Hong Kong Heritage Project, flex.co.jp).

**Blocked on the web**
- Magnum, Patrick Zachmann's 1987 photos, with captions such as "Atelier de tissage": 403. https://pro.magnumphotos.com/Package/2TYRYD1A3SGY
- SCMP 1191748 (2013), the original of the Heung Yin-king testimony: Cloudflare; Wayback offline. https://www.scmp.com/news/hong-kong/article/1191748/kowloon-walled-city-life-city-darkness
- SCMP 2127786 ("When the death knell sounded for Kowloon Walled City"), 355483 ("Shady deals colour walled city's past"), 617754 ("Hallowed halls and tainted walls", a school beside the City) and 296184: Cloudflare. Search the ids on scmp.com.
- WSJ interactive, "life" chapter: the captions of Suenn Ho's clips ("Visit a factory", "Candy factory", "Look at a well"). Wayback 503. https://web.archive.org/web/2016id_/http://projects.wsj.com/kwc/getData.php?f=grabChapter&chapter=life
- cityofdarkness.co.uk, old posts (?p=560 "A Clinic for Heroin", ?p=1228 "A Policeman's Story", ?p=791 "Government Interventions", and others): 404; Wayback offline. Probably the book's interviews.
- Blue Lotus Gallery, Girard's photo titles ("Cafe, Tung Tau Tsuen Road, 1989", "Dentist's Window, 1989", "Barbecued Meat Factory"): pages 404 or built by JavaScript. https://bluelotus-gallery.com/greg-girard-1/city-of-darkness
- *Tatler Hong Kong*, Oct 2019, "If these walls could talk", with Girard and Lambot: 403. https://www.pressreader.com/china/tatler-hong-kong/20191001/283738430946918
- Blacksmith Books, 2021, Fiona Hawthorne on her drawings of the City: 403. https://blacksmithbooks.com/2021/08/an-artist-reveals-what-life-was-like-inside-the-legendary-walled-city-of-kowloon
- Gale Primary Sources (Colonial Office papers, newspapers), shown at a CUHK workshop on 16 Jan 2025: login only. https://www.lib.cuhk.edu.hk/tc/about/events/ls-workshop-20250116-kowloon-walled-city/
- M+ collection: no keyword search, and Ryuji Miyamoto's 9 objects have generic titles only. The guide https://mplus.libguides.com/kowloon-walled-city is 404.
- A Japanese post on the City: it redirects to a Hatena login. https://reibun.hateblo.jp/entry/2019/07/19/232614
- 立場新聞 and 蘋果日報 retrospectives: both sites are closed, and the Internet Archive was offline (503) on 2026-11-05.

**Print, archives and photo collections**
- 《城寨福利會訊》, the Kai Fong newsletter, Feb 1987: primary. Not found online. 中華書局 (2026) cites it.
- *City of Darkness* (1993), pp172 and 178: photos of the Tung Tau Tsuen Road facade with about 22 named signs (Z22–Z38). The photos can give the floors. A blog of 2005 (secondary) lists the names: https://lichokiu.blogspot.com/2005/09/blog-post_112754459002873396.html
- 『九龍城探訪 完全版』 (2026), the Japanese edition of *City of Darkness Revisited*, ed. 吉田一郎: new maps and resident interviews. Print only.
- Jackie Pullinger, *Chasing the Dragon* (1980): her youth club and the school where she taught. No free text found; archive.org was offline.
- UK National Archives CO 1030/394 (police report, Mar 1953) and CO 1030/934 (Savingram, 11 Mar 1955): the Lung Kung theatre, the 16 strip-tease theatres, dog-meat shops and divans of 1952–55. Lambot quotes them (CoDR p273). Not found online.
- GRS HKRS1078-1-53, "Hsin On Centre of the Hong Kong and Macau Lutheran Church" (1987–1992): can give the address of the chapel on CoDR p252. Not digitised. https://search.grs.gov.hk/en/arcview.xhtml?eid=Kt4AVzZhTddf1jxuRF2XuQ%3D%3D
- *Sing Pao Daily News* (成報) photo archive, 1987–94 (CoDR pp337–347): street photos with readable shop and clinic signs. Not online.
- Ko Tim Keung's (高添強) photo collection (Tung Tau Tsuen Road, 1962–72): larger prints can show more signs. Not online.
- HK Government Information Services (政府新聞處) photo library: the 1975 photo of the 14-storey building on Tung Tau Tsuen Road (CoDR p336). A large copy can show the building and more signs.
- SCMP, 1993, on the landlord Chang Kat Kong (HK$34.6m for about 100 flats; an air-conditioner repair shop), quoted in CoDR p344. Paywall.
- Suenn Ho, 1992 Fulbright study (M+ CA17): the full drawings of "factories and other non-residential units by level" and the plan of each level. CoDR shows only small copies.
- Survey and Mapping Office map of 1968: a full-size copy gives every lot number and label. The copy in CoDR (pp126–127) is small.
- Girard and Lambot's negatives or the M+ prints: the address lines on the clinic signs of CoDR p8 are too small in the book.
- Fiona Hawthorne's 1986 sketches: CoDR (p210) shows two of "dozens".
- Hong Kong Yearbook 1970 (1970 年香港年鑑): addresses of dentists, for example 黃景志 at 25 Tung Tau Tsuen Rd. A Gwulo caption cites it (W1). https://gwulo.com/media/44692

**Secondary works (leads only, not sources)**
- 喬健等 (1992), pp1020–1034: a study of the residents, with the Kai Fong survey of 745 premises (1983). Print only.
- 魯金《九龍城寨史話》(1988), pp104–133: the places street by street, for example the big well at 8 Tai Chang St (大井街). Print only.
- Stephen CP Wu, "Healers in the maze", *Hong Kong Medical Journal* 2026: a survey of 72 unregistered doctors. Cloudflare. https://www.hkmj.org/node/11992 (PDF: https://www.hkmj.org/system/files/hkmj-SA202606.pdf)
- Wu Chun Pong (胡震邦), "Drilling on the edge: the unregistered dentists in post-war Kowloon Walled City", HKU MA thesis, 2025: 403. https://hub.hku.hk/handle/10722/366214
- "Kowloon Walled City Revisited: Photography and Postcoloniality", University of Michigan, with Girard's photo titles: 403. https://quod.lib.umich.edu/t/tap/7977573.0006.202
- University of Waterloo study of the links between buildings (Tramontini, Dalla-Ali, Dai, Glebe; a Vimeo talk), in CoDR pp121 and 140. No link found.

## Leads of the source search of 2026-11

The triage of the search (2026-11-17) did not make a folder for these 210 items: each one is behind a
paywall, print only, offline, or of less value. Each line gives the item, its access, the folder that
it can go in, and what the finder says that it gives (not checked).

**amo-excavation-1993-1996**
- Excavation Report of Kowloon Walled City, Hong Kong (香港九龙寨城发掘简报); Li Longlam (Li Long Lam), former curator of the Antiquities and Monuments Office; 2007. Access: print-only. Planned folder: amo-excavation-1993-1996. Gives: The excavation of 1994: the stone plaques, the gate foundations and the other finds, with drawings (INFERRED from the kind of report). https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Li_Long_Lam
- Kowloon Walled City. (九龍寨城); Hong Kong. Government Secretariat. Recreation and Culture Branch. Antiquities and Monuments Office (古物古蹟辦事處); c.1995. Access: print-only. Planned folder: amo-excavation-1993-1996. Gives: The AMO's public leaflet on the City, made just after the excavation and the building of the park (INFERRED: the history, the South Gate and Yamen finds, with colour pictures). Content not seen. https://julac-hku.primo.exlibrisgroup.com/discovery/fulldisplay?docid=alma991000373049703414&vid=852JULAC_HKU:HKU

**building-and-demolition-records-1968-1997**
- Organic demolition: Walled City comes down; Angela Tam; Asian Architect & Contractor, v.23, no.6, pp. 34-40; 1993. Access: print-only. Planned folder: building-and-demolition-records-1968-1997. Gives: By the index abstract: the Walled City, an example of organic architecture, was under demolition, and the article examines how the unstable structure was taken down (1993). Illustrated. https://hkapi.lib.cuhk.edu.hk/handle/msO8s77aBT/2628
- Conservation and Walled City; Jane Ram; Asian Architect & Contractor, v.21, no.11, pp. 18-20; 1991. Access: print-only. Planned folder: building-and-demolition-records-1968-1997. Gives: By the abstract: in Kowloon the '50s tenements of the Walled City were being pulled down, not for new buildings, but so that the old buildings could be restored (1991). Exact phrase: "pulling down the '50s tenements not https://hkapi.lib.cuhk.edu.hk/handle/msO8s77aBT/12921
- Projects; Building Journal Hong Kong China, Dec 1992, pp. 32-43; 1992-12. Access: print-only. Planned folder: building-and-demolition-records-1968-1997. Gives: A news item of December 1992, 'Kowloon Walled City coming down slowly', in a list of 22 project reports. It falls just after the award of the demolition contract (November 1992, see item 30). https://hkapi.lib.cuhk.edu.hk/handle/msO8s77aBT/8259
- ASD scores international prize for park design; Building Journal Hong Kong China, Feb 1994, pp. 32-33; 1994-02. Access: print-only. Planned folder: building-and-demolition-records-1968-1997. Gives: By the abstract: the Chinese garden keeps the memories of the Walled City; consultants from mainland China designed the pavilions; a plan to build a replica of the Walled City was dropped (1994). https://hkapi.lib.cuhk.edu.hk/handle/msO8s77aBT/1125
- Kowloon Walled City Park: spirit and form; Steven Rose; Building Journal Hong Kong China, Oct 1995, pp. 36-41; 1995-10. Access: print-only. Planned folder: building-and-demolition-records-1968-1997. Gives: By the abstract: the completion ceremony of 6 September 1995 ended a five-year project of the Architectural Services Department; an interview with the senior architect Tse Shun Kai; eight features (a yamen yard, pavilion https://hkapi.lib.cuhk.edu.hk/handle/msO8s77aBT/49
- Kowloon Walled City Park; HKIA journal (the official journal of the Hong Kong Institute of Architects), no.4/Special, pp. 118-123; 1996. Access: print-only. Planned folder: building-and-demolition-records-1968-1997. Gives: A six-page project description of the park with maps (1996). **Other**
- Not done in lens gap-campus-socialwork-press: 華僑日報 and 工商日報 page scans: I read only the archive.org keyword and headline lists. The OCR text of NPWK19860610 is garbled. Read the page scans of each issue below to get the article text.
- Not done in lens gap-crime-drugs-records: Wah Kiu Yat Po (華僑日報) issues of 1984-1993 whose keyword lists hold 城寨/城砦 and a drug word (111 issues): only 11 were read. Most matches are clearance news. The other 100 are not checked.
- Not done in lens gap-campus-socialwork-press: HKBU journalism papers (The Young Reporter, 新報人): the HKBU archive (archive.lib.hkbu.edu.hk) and its search give 403; the news page on the archive (comm.hkbu.edu.hk) refused the connection.
- Not done in lens gap-clearance-from-below: Annual reports and newsletters of the Hong Kong Christian Industrial Committee (香港基督教工業委員會) for 1987-1993: no web copy found by search, and no record in the HKU catalogue for the committee together with the City. I did not search the HKBU or CUHK special collections for the committee's own archive, because I used my search budget first on the newspaper and TV leads.
- Not done in lens gap-clearance-from-below: Catholic Justice and Peace Commission (天主教香港教區正義和平委員會): hkjp.org gave HTTP 403 to WebFetch. Its statements of 1987-1993 were not checked.
- Not done in lens gap-crime-drugs-records: Rod A. L. Mason's 1985 report: not found. The Austin Macauley page of his book and the SCMP review gave 403. The GRS and the police reviews were not searched for 'Mason' by name.
- Not done in lens gap-crime-drugs-records: Kung Sheung Daily News (工商日報) issues NPKS19720113 and NPKS19741108: not read (time).
- Not done in lens gap-daily-services-departments: Annual departmental reports of 1975-1993 of the Water Supplies, Fire Services, Urban Services, Education, Social Welfare and Post Office departments: no online copy found. The HKU Digital Repository holds government reports to about 1973 only; archive.org holds the Medical and Health reports to 1972-73 only (Wellcome scans). Print copies are in HK libraries.
- Not done in lens gap-daily-services-departments: China Light and Power annual reports and the CLP Heritage Centre: clpgroup.com gives HTTP 403; hongkongheritage.org (the CLP oral-history archive) gives HTTP 403; archive.org has no CLP annual report of 1970-1995 by title. Not read.
- Not done in lens gap-daily-services-departments: archive.org NPWK (華僑日報) issues of 1975-1991: 315 issues name 城寨 or 城砦, but the description field is a long keyword list, not a list of headlines, so single reports on fire, water or refuse could not be isolated with WebFetch. Not done.
- Not done in lens gap-district-board-housing-authority: Urban Council Proceedings 1987-1993 (park, hawkers, food factories): the only online copy found is on hkintexts (403); not read.
- Not done in lens gap-foreign-free-press: Norwegian National Library (api.nb.no), found during this lens: I did not read the open items Haugesunds Avis 1987-12-07 p.7, Jærbladet 1988-03-21 pp.11-13, Utsyn 1984 Nr 2 and 18, 1985 Nr 13 and 37/38, 1987 Nr 17, 1988, and the NLM yearbooks for 1989, 1991 and 1993. Each one is open (CC BY-NC-ND) and can be read through the ALTO endpoint, page by page. Items marked LIBRARY or NB (Aftenposten, Stavanger Aftenblad, VG, Vårt Land, Dagen, Søndag-Søndag) are readable only from a Norwegian library or at the NB.
- Not done in lens gap-named-persons-records: Hong Kong Who's Who and the honours lists of the HK Government Gazette / London Gazette for the District Officers and Housing Department clearance staff: not done; the WebSearch budget of 15 was used up before this step, and no catalogue API was known.
- Not done in lens gap-named-persons-records: Michael Suen (孫明揚) obituary and own later interviews: not searched (budget). zh.wikipedia cites an obituary (am730, 2024) and a funeral notice (Headline Daily); not opened.
- Not done in lens gap-named-persons-records: British Diplomatic Oral History Programme (Churchill College) for Sir John Boyd and Richard Margolis, both named by Gordon Jones: the old chu.cam.ac.uk paths give 404 and the archive site search does not list the programme.
- Not done in lens gap-street-address-search: Hong Kong $ Directory 1983, 1985, 1987, 1988 (University of Toronto scans on archive.org): the in-item text search (inside.php) gave 404 on the ca.archive.org server, and the djvu.txt files (1,116 pages) are too large to read with WebFetch. Full-text search found no street of the City in any edition. A page-by-page check of the Kowloon City listings is not done.
- Not done in lens top-blocked-a: Read the Wayback copies that the availability API confirms (The News Lens 98486 and 98591, Crossing 18963, Asia Society 2024-05-23 and 2025-03-20, the Tsang thesis PDF of 2016-08-15, Washington Post 1991, SCMP 16808, 54450 and 3263596, HK Heritage Project 22115 and 22246, Granta 2025-03-22, flex.co.jp 1999-02-19): WebFetch refuses web.archive.org. Open them in a browser.
- Not done in lens top-blocked-a: View the other 51 Pete Macklin photos of Oct 1986 for a view of the City: not done; only one was viewed.
- Not done in lens top-blocked-b: A second search for a copy of the RTHK documentary 'Kowloon Walled City Stories' (1988): not done, search cap.
- Not done in lens top-blocked-b: The 1981 Census and the 1991 Census (the City was still full in March 1991) on censtatd.gov.hk: the product numbers are not known and the C&SD product pages need JavaScript; search cap.
- Not done in lens top-blocked-b: The 1986 by-census Main Report and the Tertiary Planning Unit tabulations on censtatd.gov.hk: a search names them, but no PDF link was found; product pages need JavaScript.

Not done: the file is larger than the 10 MB limit of WebFetch, and downloads with curl are not permitted in this session. Download it in a browser and filter it locally.
- Not done in lens top-books: Offbeat special edition supplement 5 (Hong Kong Police, Chinese) and the red-publish preview PDF of 舊屋 (books-12): over the 10 MB limit of WebFetch; open them in a browser.

- Not done in lens top-china: 中央日報 of the other days of Dec 1947 to Feb 1948 (for example 1947-12-10, 1948-01-06, -09, -17, -20, -21, -24, -28): not read, for the same reason. Left for the collection step.
- Not done in lens top-china: 申報 1948.01 volumes (shenbao-1948.01-92 to -119), 新聞日報 (南昌) and the other 新聞報 issues of Jan 1948: not read; only 新聞報 1948-01-17 page 2 was read.
- Not done in lens top-china: 籌辦夷務始末 道光朝 卷七十六: the volume is narrowed to 冊66-68, but the page with 耆英's memorial on 九龍 is not found; the OCR cannot be searched. Left for page-by-page reading (see top-china-10).
- Not done in lens top-commons: Commons: view the 1936 War Office original and the 1960 War Office map (top-commons-5, -7) at Kowloon City. Reason: the files are 38.7 MB and 78.6 MB; WebFetch cannot get them at full size, and a thumbnail is too small. Use a browser with the Commons zoom viewer.
- Not done in lens top-depictions: Peter Yung's book The Dream of a Lost Traveller: The Films & Photography of Peter Yung (HKFA, 2015): does it print his photos of the City, 1974-75? Not checked.
- Not done in lens top-film: AP Archive YouTube channel (and British Movietone, which AP holds): search for clips of the 14 Jan 1987 announcement, the 1991-92 evictions and the 1993-94 demolition. One search found nothing; the follow-up searches were stopped by the 5-hour limit.

- Not done in lens top-gap-tv-news-archives: Hong Kong Film Archive (香港電影資料館) collection search https://mcms.lcsd.gov.hk/Search/search/enquire?&request_locale=en_US : the page does not show its form field names, thus I could not send a query.

The record of the film, if it exists, is hidden.

**press-and-agency-photos-1948-1995**
- The notorious Kowloon Walled City, with 40,000 residents, is going to be torn down in three years time. 14JAN87; Post Staff Photographer, South China Morning Post (Getty Images, SCMP collection); 1987-01-14. Access: paywall. Planned folder: press-and-agency-photos-1948-1995. Gives: A view of the City on 14 Jan 1987, the day of the clearance announcement and survey. The caption gives the 1987 press figure of 40,000 residents and a three-year timetable. https://www.gettyimages.ie/detail/news-photo/notorious-walled-city-news-photo/1096406140
- Walled City - Old Man reading clearance notice put on by officials. 14 January 1987.; Sam Chan, South China Morning Post (Getty Images); 1987-01-14. Access: paywall. Planned folder: press-and-agency-photos-1948-1995. Gives: A resident reads the clearance notice that officials posted on 14 Jan 1987. A larger copy can show the text of the notice and the place where it was posted. https://www.gettyimages.ie/detail/news-photo/old-man-news-photo/1124850391
- For Hong Kong Story - Walled City - Kowloon Walled City Phase II clearance. An assailant being carried away by ambulance-man, B/W photo. 07 April 1992.; Wan Kam-yan, South China Morning Post (Getty Images); 1992-04-07. Access: paywall. Planned folder: press-and-agency-photos-1948-1995. Gives: Violence at the Phase II clearance on 7 Apr 1992: ambulance crew carry an assailant away. It dates the Phase II clearance. https://www.gettyimages.ie/detail/news-photo/x/1124850434
- HONG KONG-WALLED CITY EVACUEE; Manuel Ceneta, AFP (Getty Images); 1992-04-07. Access: paywall. Planned folder: press-and-agency-photos-1948-1995. Gives: Two AFP photos of 7 Apr 1992. Chiu Shek Yuen, a former resident, is arrested after he tried to saw off the padlock of a repossessed unit. The caption says five people were wounded as the government completed the latest p https://www.gettyimages.ie/detail/news-photo/x/1258855330
- Kowloon Walled City. 29 Jun 91; Post Staff Photographer, South China Morning Post (Getty Images); 1991-06-29. Access: paywall. Planned folder: press-and-agency-photos-1948-1995. Gives: A view of the City on 29 Jun 1991, before the Phase I clearance of late 1991. The caption gives no detail; the image is the value. https://www.gettyimages.ie/detail/news-photo/x/1125461423
- The Kowloon Walled City.; Wan Kam-yan, South China Morning Post (Getty Images); 1993-03-20. Access: paywall. Planned folder: press-and-agency-photos-1948-1995. Gives: The City on 20 Mar 1993 (Getty 'date created'), just before the demolition of 23 Mar 1993. https://www.gettyimages.ie/detail/news-photo/x/1126363276
- Demolition of the Kowloon Walled City.; Robert Ng, South China Morning Post (Getty Images); 1993-03-23. Access: paywall. Planned folder: press-and-agency-photos-1948-1995. Gives: The start of the demolition on 23 Mar 1993. https://www.gettyimages.ie/detail/news-photo/demolition-of-the-kowloon-walled-city-news-photo/1126363008
- Kowloon Walled City demolition with Lion Rock at the background. 30 April 1993; Paul Lakatos, South China Morning Post (Getty Images); 1993-04-30. Access: paywall. Planned folder: press-and-agency-photos-1948-1995. Gives: The demolition five weeks in, on 30 Apr 1993, seen with Lion Rock behind. It can show the order in which the blocks came down. https://www.gettyimages.ie/detail/news-photo/x/1125461420
- For Hong Kong Story - Walled City - Former walled city resident Chan Hee-yuen, 28 April 1993.; Eric Li, South China Morning Post (Getty Images); 1993-04-28. Access: paywall. Planned folder: press-and-agency-photos-1948-1995. Gives: A named former resident, Chan Hee-yuen, on 28 Apr 1993. The SCMP story that it went with can give her or his account; find it in the HKU clippings. https://www.gettyimages.ie/detail/news-photo/x/1124850406
- Walled City - A resident that used to live in the Kowloon Walled City but now this is where she lives. 09 June 1993; Jeremy Piper, South China Morning Post (Getty Images); 1993-06-09. Access: paywall. Planned folder: press-and-agency-photos-1948-1995. Gives: The rehousing of a former resident, 9 Jun 1993. It does not show the City itself. https://www.gettyimages.ie/detail/news-photo/x/1124850392
- An overview of Kowloon Walled City, probably at the end of the 1980s.; Post Staff Photographer, South China Morning Post (Getty Images); c.1988. Access: paywall. Planned folder: press-and-agency-photos-1948-1995. Gives: A general view of the whole City, late 1980s. The image can be compared with the frozen structure. https://www.gettyimages.ie/detail/news-photo/x/1126367605
- A man makes noodles in a sweet shop in the Walled City, Kowloon, Hong Kong.; Gerhard Joren, LightRocket (Getty Images); 1990-08-01. Access: paywall. Planned folder: press-and-agency-photos-1948-1995. Gives: Food work inside the City in 1990: a man makes noodles in a sweet shop. An interior of a shop at peak density. https://www.gettyimages.ie/detail/news-photo/x/453205918
- Children Play on Unfenced Skyscraper Rooftop in Hong Kong (and four more Kowloon Walled City photos by Nik Wheeler); Nik Wheeler, Corbis Historical (Getty Images); c.1979-1980. Access: paywall. Planned folder: press-and-agency-photos-1948-1995. Gives: Five photos: children on an unfenced roof (1219604119), the entrance (1219604125), caged balconies (1219603974), a boy flying a kite among TV aerials on a roof near Kai Tak (1217531331), and dense blocks (1217531320). Th https://www.gettyimages.ie/detail/news-photo/x/1219604119
- Residents of the Kowloon Walled City getting water from a standpipe for cooking and washing clothes. 06OCT77 (and three more of the same day); C. Y. Yu, South China Morning Post (Getty Images); 1977-10-06. Access: paywall. Planned folder: press-and-agency-photos-1948-1995. Gives: Water in 1977: residents use a standpipe for cooking and washing clothes (1096061400), set up illegal water pipes (1096061434), the illegal pipes (1096061456), and youngsters make 'water bombs' (1096061474). https://www.gettyimages.ie/detail/news-photo/x/1096061400

- Chasing the Dragon (revised edition: Chasing the Dragon: One Woman's Struggle Against the Darkness of Hong Kong's Drug Dens); Jackie Pullinger (潘靈卓) with Andrew Quicke; Hodder & Stoughton (1980); Baker Books / Chosen (2007); 1980. Access: print-only. Planned folder: pullinger-circle-1966-2026. - 追龍（增訂版）; Jackie Pullinger (潘靈卓) and Andrew Quicke (桂爾克); translator 黃大業; 高示; 2001-09-01. Access: print-only. Planned folder: pullinger-circle-1966-2026. Chapters: https://www.mezu.com.tw/products/%E8%BF%BD%E9%BE%8D-%E5%A2%9E%E8%A8%82%E7%89%88-
- Women Who Chase Butterflies; Louisa Wong (memoir); c.2023. Access: print-only. Planned folder: pullinger-circle-1966-2026. Gives: Her childhood in the City and the move to public housing, then her career (Harvard Business School, Wall Street, a recruitment firm in China in 1996). The SCMP extract calls the City in her book 'a place of grit, hope an https://www.scmp.com/lifestyle/family-relationships/article/3219200/growing-next-prostitutes-and-drug-addicts-hong-kongs-walled-city-recruitment-pioneer-louisa-wong
- 追蝶人生：黃慧儀自傳; 黃慧儀; 商務印書館（香港）有限公司; 2024-06-18. Access: print-only. Planned folder: pullinger-circle-1966-2026. - God's Heart for the Poor; Philippa Stroud (with Christine Leonard); Kingsway Communications, Eastbourne; 1999. Access: print-only. Planned folder: pullinger-circle-1966-2026. Gives: First-person memories of visits to the Walled City and friendships with people there, and the approach of the planes low over the City to Kai Tak. Her years in Hong Kong are about the late 1980s (not checked in the text) https://openlibrary.org/works/OL9687784W

- Report of a Survey on the Social Environment of Inhabitants of the Kowloon Walled City; N. W. S. Chow; Institute for Social Work Training, Hong Kong; 1975. Access: print-only. Planned folder: surveys-and-studies-1960-1995. Gives: By its title, the homes, families, work and social needs of the residents in 1975. Content not seen. https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC5732597/
- 九龍城寨居民的生活狀况; Nelson W. S. Chow (周永新); The Hong Kong Journal of Social Work (香港社會工作學報); 1976 (summer issue). Access: print-only. Planned folder: surveys-and-studies-1960-1995. Gives: A survey of 97 households in the City in 1975, made for the Salvation Army (救世軍), which then ran a primary school and a clinic in the City; 45 of the households had used its services. - Field work report, CUHK Department of Anthropology, A4-38b (九龍城砦的居民); Chinese University of Hong Kong (香港中文大學), Department of Anthropology; 喬健等; 1989. Access: print-only. Planned folder: surveys-and-studies-1960-1995. Gives: The residents of the City in 1988-89 (social life and customs, buildings), in 49 leaves of text and a volume of photographic slides, made in the first years of the clearance. - 九龍城砦的居民 (in 《羅香林教授紀念論文集》, 下冊, p. 1001); 喬健, 蘇崇尹, 林進光, 梁礎安; volume ed. 珠海文史研究所學會; 臺北: 新文豐出版; 1992. Access: print-only. Planned folder: surveys-and-studies-1960-1995. **tna-files-kwc-1898-1991**
- FCO 21/1268: Kowloon Walled City of China, Hong Kong: environmental and political considerations; Foreign and Commonwealth Office, Far Eastern Department (registered file FEH 3/301/2); 1974. Access: paywall. Planned folder: tna-files-kwc-1898-1991. Gives: Conditions in the City in 1974: the Adam Matthew keywords name flooding, housing and health. The file has plans and a photograph. It is the FCO file of the year of the House of Lords debate that the repo holds. https://beta.nationalarchives.gov.uk/catalogue/id/C11222821/
- FCO 21/1139: Kowloon Walled City of China in Hong Kong; Foreign and Commonwealth Office, Far Eastern Department (registered file FEH 3/301/2); 1973-01-01 to 1974-01-25. Access: paywall. Planned folder: tna-files-kwc-1898-1991. Gives: The UK view of the City in 1973. The Adam Matthew keywords name electricity, water, flooding, health, opium, education, labour and the press. https://beta.nationalarchives.gov.uk/catalogue/id/C11161805/

**world-press-1899-1995**

- The Forgotten City; Philip Norman, The Sunday Times Magazine (London); 1974-06-09. Access: paywall. Planned folder: world-press-1899-1995. ### Leads of the reading of 2026-11: closed or not found

Records that the items of the reading cite and that are not open online: in an archive, in print, behind a login, or not found. Each line gives the lead id, the lead, and where it is.

- L0017: Housing Department survey notices of 14 Jan 1987 and its Special Duties office at Block 17, Lok Fu Estate (named). Where: Government Records Service (GRS, 政府檔案處), Search Room, Hong Kong Public Records Building, Kwun Tong; files read on site under the Public Records (Access) Rules 1996; the online catalogue gives no scope note and no digital copy: HKRS1077-1-2 'BULLETIN' (14.1.1987-10.12.1987, CNTA Special Duties), https://search.grs.gov.hk/en/arcview.xhtml?eid=%2BltII0A3R12gNJkJdDKUiQ%3D%3D ; HKRS1078-1-11 'SDA'S MEETING' (13.1.1987-28.1.1987); HKRS1078-1-62 'INTERVIEWS AND VISITS' (7.1.1987-29.5.1992). All three are listed in the repo's GRS CSVs (catalogue rows only). (cited by top-china-1, china-official-records-1933-1987)
- L0018: Statement of the 九龍城砦街坊福利事業促進委員會, 14 Jan 1987 (quoted in part). Where: not found: The full text of the statement of 九龍城砦街坊福利事業促進委員會 of 14 Jan 1987 was not found. The repo holds two partial prints: Ta Kung Pao (大公報) 15 Jan 1987, NPTKP19870115 leaf n4 (china-official-records-1933-1987/top-china-1, the citing item) and Wah Kiu Yat Po (華僑日報) 15 Jan 1987, NPWK19870115 leaf n8 (hk-chin (cited by top-china-1, china-official-records-1933-1987)
- L0059: 《城寨福利會訊》, February 1987: the newsletter that counts visits to the City clinics since the 1970s (cited through 陳惠芬 2008). Where: not found: 《城寨福利會訊》, Feb 1987 (the newsletter of the kaifong association). The repo knows it only through 陳惠芬 (2008), cited by the CUHK clinics book and the 中華書局 history (p. 96, note 38). I found no copy in an open archive (an archive.org search matches single characters and gives no useful hit). INFERRED: a c (cited by missions-16, church-and-mission-histories)
- L0076: 劉知三, 〈恩光老人中心〉, 《中華傳道會五十週年金禧特刊》 (香港: 中華傳道會, 1993), pp. 50-51; and 劉知三, 〈恩光堂〉, same book, p. 32. Where: Print only (1993): 基督教中華傳道會 (CNEC) Hong Kong and theological libraries; no catalogue record confirmed. (cited by repo-audit-17, church-and-mission-histories)
- L0085: AFI Catalog of Feature Films, Bloodsport (1988), https://catalog.afi.com/Catalog/moviedetails/60095 : the source of the shoot start date 17 Oct 1986; it can give the end of the shoot and thus a narrower date for the City scenes. Where: AFI Catalog of Feature Films, Bloodsport (1988), https://catalog.afi.com/Catalog/moviedetails/60095: free in a normal browser, but behind a bot check for tools. (cited by depictions-1, depictions-film-and-tv-1966-2025)
- L0095: The Dragon Dynasty DVD (2007) of Crime Story has deleted scenes (en Wikipedia): they can hold more City footage of mid-1992. Where: Crime Story (重案組), Dragon Dynasty DVD (2007): retail. (cited by depictions-4, depictions-film-and-tv-1966-2025)
- L0104: Yellowthread Street, episode 7 'Rummy's Cut' (ITV, 24 Feb 1990): the full City sequence, if a legal copy can be found. Where: Yellowthread Street, episode 7 'Rummy's Cut' (Yorkshire Television for ITV, broadcast 24 Feb 1990): ITV / Yorkshire Television archive; a home-video release can exist (not checked). (cited by depictions-7, depictions-film-and-tv-1966-2025)
- L0117: TBS, ギミア・ぶれいく, episode 128 (a TBS programme of about 1989-1992, inferred from general knowledge of the series; date of the episode not given): footage of City residents, children with a Famicom, and an interview with a man whose family had lived in the City for generations. Ask TBS (TBS総合資料室 or its archive sales) for the date. Where: TBS Television (TBSテレビ), its film library (TBS映像倉庫) and archive sales; no public copy or public catalogue of the episode found (cited by top-gap-tv-news-archives-5, documentaries-video-and-podcasts-1990-2026)
- L0129: TCM (https://tcm.com/tcmdb/title/434915/baraka) and AFI Catalog (https://catalog.afi.com/Catalog/moviedetails/59459, 403 to my tool) are reported by a search summary to give the Baraka shoot window (start 10 Aug or 1 Sep 1990, end late 1991). Check them to date the City shots. Where: AFI Catalog of Feature Films, https://catalog.afi.com/Catalog/moviedetails/59459 (exists; behind a Cloudflare bot check). The TCM page is retired. (cited by depictions-8, film-and-tv-footage-1958-1994)
- L0138: Government Civil Engineering Department contract for the demolition of the Walled City (Express Builders - Cleveland Wrecking joint venture), 1993. Where: Civil Engineering Department (土木工程署, now CEDD) contract records, not in the GRS online catalogue; nearest GRS file HKRS439-11-2 'Demolition of Kowloon Walled City' (12.5.1992-24.6.1994, original ref.
